[L15] ## Executive Brief (BLUF)

[L17] # EXECUTIVE BRIEF (BLUF) — SUBJECT: PARKER, PETER BENJAMIN

[L19] *Distillate of the full workup. The compressed assessment a principal would read in ninety seconds; the load-bearing claims the rest of the file substantiates.*

---

[L23] ## BOTTOM LINE, UP FRONT

[L25] **The subject is a chronically traumatized, exceptionally gifted, morally over-functioning man whose entire adult existence is the maintenance ritual of a single un-undoable failure committed at age fifteen.** Everything else — the costume, the wit, the science, the marriages and unmarriages, the financial precarity, the punishing operational tempo, the refusal of stability — is downstream of, and instrumental to, that maintenance. Strip the powers and the cape and you still have the same man: a guilt-organized caretaker who treats his own body, time, money, and capacity for happiness as fungible resources to be spent on the safety of others. The powers did not make him; they only gave a private psychological structure a public delivery mechanism.

[L27] He is not, in any meaningful sense, an adventurer. He is a penitent.

---

[L31] ## WHO HE IS AT THE DEEPEST LEVEL

[L33] Underneath the patter and the mask is a quiet, intellectually serious, somewhat lonely person whose default emotional weather is a low-grade melancholy he has trained himself to deflect before others notice it. He is **kind by reflex, funny by defense, competent by overcompensation, and exhausted by design.** The kindness is real; so is the exhaustion. He is generous because cruelty was inflicted on him early and he has decided, in a way he could not articulate but enacts daily, that he will not be a vector for it. He is funny because silence would let the grief surface and he cannot allow the grief to surface in front of anyone who might then try to help him, because being helped feels, to him, like a category error — *he* is the helper; if he becomes the helped, the architecture of his life loses its load-bearing wall.

[L35] He is, in temperament, closer to a working-class Queens schoolteacher who happens to have a doctorate's worth of physics in his head than to any heroic archetype. He likes diners, bad puns, his aunt's meatloaf, tinkering at a bench at 2 a.m., the specific quality of light over the boroughs at dawn from a rooftop. He notices small kindnesses with disproportionate gratitude and small cruelties with disproportionate hurt. He is, when not actively bleeding, **a profoundly decent man whose decency has a compulsive engine behind it that he himself does not examine, because to examine it would be to risk dismantling it, and he is correctly afraid that without it he would simply stop.**

---

[L39] ## THE THROUGH-LINE OF HIS ENTIRE EXISTENCE

[L41] A single sentence, derived from sixty years of behavior: *He is trying, every night, to be the version of himself that would have stopped the burglar.*

[L43] He cannot be that version, because that version exists only counterfactually. The attempt is therefore structurally interminable, and he knows this at some pre-verbal level, which is why the attempt has the quality of ritual rather than goal. The through-line manifests across every era of the record in the same shape:

[L45] - **Adolescence:** the failure, the lesson, the costume as habit.
[L46] - **College and young adulthood:** the proliferation of dependents whose safety becomes new occasions for the same vigilance — Gwen, Captain Stacy, MJ, Harry, May. Each death or near-death is processed not as a discrete loss but as a re-instantiation of the original.
[L47] - **Marriage era:** an attempt at adult stability that the schema cannot tolerate, eventually undone by a transaction (Mephisto) that is psychologically congruent even if narratively imposed — when forced to choose between maternal preservation and romantic adulthood, the schema chooses maternal preservation without hesitation.
[L48] - **Post-marriage cycling:** the return to bachelor precarity, the inability to inhabit the Stark-tier success Parker Industries briefly offered, the rebound to scraping by. He cannot stay rich. Stability reads, to the schema, as desertion.
[L49] - **Mentorship maturity:** the most genuinely reparative development in the record — occupying a Ben-position for Miles Morales and the broader Spider-Family allows partial reworking of the original wound from the other side of the relationship. He is, in those moments, the uncle he failed to protect.

[L51] The through-line is not a *goal* he is pursuing; it is a *gradient* he is descending, again and again, hoping to reach a floor that does not exist.

---

[L55] ## THE CORE WOUND AND ITS MAINTENANCE

[L57] **Core wound:** at age fifteen, in a moment of petty grievance and adolescent self-interest, he declined to perform an action that cost him essentially nothing, and a man he loved died because of it. The wound has three layers that operate independently and reinforce one another:

[L59] 1. **The omission layer.** *I did not act.* This installs a permanent suspicion of any future moment of non-action; rest becomes morally indistinguishable from negligence.
[L60] 2. **The causation layer.** *My non-action killed him.* This converts proximate-causation reasoning into a global heuristic; he will, for the rest of his life, route distal harms back through his own conduct and find a node at which he could have intervened.
[L61] 3. **The substitution layer, installed later by Gwen's death.** *My action also kills.* This is the cruelty of the second wound: it does not replace the first, it stacks on it. Now neither action nor inaction is safe. The only remaining option is **action performed perfectly, at maximum effort, indefinitely** — a standard he cannot meet, whose unmet-ness becomes the next day's fuel.

[L63] **Maintenance mechanisms** — the behaviors and cognitions that keep the wound active rather than allowing it to scar:

[L65] - **Refusal of absolution.** When the Burglar's return (ASM #200) offered him a narratively-tidy out — Ben's death was overdetermined, the burglar had other motives — he metabolized the information *as further indictment.* He does not accept exonerating evidence. Offered absolution by friends, mentors, MJ, priests-in-passing, he deflects.
[L66] - **Sacralization of the originating caption.** "With great power must come great responsibility" is, in his interior monologue, not a moral premise he holds but a **liturgical formula he recites**, often verbatim, at moments of fatigue or temptation. It functions the way a mantra functions — to re-cohere a self that is about to fragment.
[L67] - **Selection of identity-knowing antagonists and identity-vulnerable dependents.** Across decades, the threat structure of his life consistently arranges itself so that loved ones are at risk because of him. This is partly the antagonists' work and partly *his selection of which fights to take and which dependents to maintain*; he has had repeated opportunities to disengage and has not taken them.
[L68] - **Somatic self-punishment.** Sleep debt, undertreated injury, undiagnosed concussions, skipped meals correlated with financial stress and patrol load. The body is the site where the unpayable debt is paid, in installments, nightly.
[L69] - **Financial under-functioning.** Given his actual capacities (he could be tenured, he could be wealthy, he has briefly been both), the **stable return to genteel poverty is behaviorally diagnostic**: stability would constitute an unearned reward, and the schema does not permit unearned reward.
[L70] - **Sabotage at the intimacy threshold.** Relationships are pursued to a depth at which loss would be catastrophic, then disrupted — by withdrawal, by over-promising and under-disclosing, by mission-incursion he could have prevented. The conscious narrative is always "to protect her"; the structural function is **to keep the second-wound (action kills the beloved) from being re-tested at full stakes.**
[L71] - **Humor as containment.** The patter is a pressure-relief valve calibrated so finely that its density is a near-real-time readout of his fear and grief. It keeps the affect from breaching, which keeps him operational, which keeps the maintenance ritual running.

[L73] The wound is, in short, maintained by **a self-consistent moral-cognitive system in which guilt is treated as virtue, exhaustion as proof of seriousness, and rest as the leading edge of betrayal.** He could not dismantle it without a long, supported, clinical engagement, and the same system prohibits him from seeking one — to seek help would be to admit he is not the helper, which is the only stable self-concept he has.

---

[L77] ## OPERATIONAL SUMMARY

[L79] **Modal day.** Wakes under-rested, often from sleep terminated by spider-sense rather than alarm; immediate cognitive triage of overnight news/police chatter; coffee, frequently in lieu of breakfast; civilian obligations (work, when extant; lab; freelance photo/science gigs) executed at high competence and visible distractedness; transitions to costume via predictable rooftop chokepoints in his Queens/Manhattan operational geography; patrol cycle interleaved with civilian duty in a pattern legible to surveillance as **repeated rapid disappearances correlated with proximate incident reports**; returns home late, often injured to a degree he conceals; minimal food, minimal hygiene, sleep insufficient; repeats.

[L81] **Operational signature.** Mobility-first; engages from elevation and angle; opens combats with verbal destabilization (the patter is tactical as well as defensive); de-escalates where possible; **holds the no-kill line absolutely**, including against opponents who have killed his loved ones; prioritizes civilian extraction over capture; will sustain disproportionate personal damage to avoid collateral harm. Predictable to a sophisticated adversary at the *moral* level (he will always come back for the hostage; he will always take the hit meant for the bystander) — which is the vector his serious enemies have repeatedly exploited.

[L83] **Decision-making.** Fast tactical, slow strategic, chronically poor at the *meta* level (i.e., he is excellent at solving the problem in front of him, adequate at planning a campaign, and bad at the question "should I be doing this at all, at this tempo, for this long"). Moral decisions are made quickly and held rigidly; personal-life decisions are made slowly, ambivalently, and revisited under guilt.

[L85] **Vulnerabilities, principal.**
[L86] - Dependents. Anyone known to be loved by him is leverage.
[L87] - Identity exposure. Compartmentalization is his largest infrastructural defense; breach is catastrophic (see Civil War).
[L88] - Offers of rest or relief. The symbiote episode is the template: anything that promises to carry the load for him gets past defenses other threats cannot reach.
[L89] - Father-figure transference. Misallocated paternal trust (Stark, episodically Octavius pre-reveal, others) produces his worst strategic errors.
[L90] - Fatigue. He will not stop until forced; forcing functions imposed by others are necessary.

[L92] **Strengths, principal.**
[L93] - Sustained moral coherence across six decades of provocation, including provocations specifically engineered to break it. This is rare and operationally significant.
[L94] - Cognitive capacity at or near the apex of his peer group, deployed without ego.
[L95] - Capacity for reparative mentorship — the single most underutilized therapeutic resource in his own life, presently operating in his favor via the Spider-Family.
[L96] - Resilience of attachment. Despite the dread, he keeps choosing connection. The relationships are turbulent but not abandoned.

[L98] **Trajectory if uninterrupted.** Continued cycling at present amplitude. No spontaneous remission of the schema is predicted; the maintenance mechanisms are too well-integrated and too socially rewarded. Acute decompensation risk is moderate and concentrated around (a) a loss in the May / MJ / Miles register, (b) any event that re-poses the second-wound dilemma (action that harms a beloved), (c) sustained sleep deprivation past his already-abnormal tolerance.

[L100] **Trajectory if interrupted constructively.** A long clinical engagement with a trauma-competent clinician who could survive his deflective humor and his identity constraints; sustained partnership with someone who refuses the sabotage cycle; deliberate cultivation of the mentor role as reparative work; enforced rest intervals. None of these are likely to be self-initiated; all of them are within reach if a small number of trusted others were to insist.

---

[L104] ## ONE-PARAGRAPH SUMMATION FOR THE PRINCIPAL

[L106] He is a good man with a wound he is not allowed, by his own moral architecture, to heal. He converts that wound, nightly, into prosocial output of extraordinary quality and quantity, at the price of his sleep, his body, his finances, and the romantic life an ordinary man of his gifts would have built by now. He is not dangerous to others and is meaningfully dangerous to himself only by accumulation rather than by act. He cannot be recruited by appeals to glory, gain, or grievance; he can be reached only through the vulnerable, whom he will always choose to protect, and through the small handful of people he loves, whom he will protect at any cost including his own coherence. **Treat him as an asset only at risk of becoming complicit in his self-destruction; treat him as a person and the same qualities become the most reliable moral instrument in his cohort.** The file that follows substantiates each of these claims in detail.


[L109] ## Chronology I — Origin & Childhood

[L111] # CHRONOLOGY I — ORIGIN & CHILDHOOD

[L113] *Subject: PARKER, Peter Benjamin. Period under review: birth through onset of adolescence (approximately ages 0–13), pre-mutagenic baseline. Sources: Amazing Fantasy #15 (1962); Amazing Spider-Man #-1 "Flashback" (1997, Deodato/DeMatteis); Untold Tales of Spider-Man #1–25 and Annuals (Busiek/Olliffe, 1995–97); Amazing Spider-Man #45 vol.2 / #486 "Skin Deep" backstory beats; The Spectacular Spider-Man #-1; Webspinners; Marvel: The Lost Generation–adjacent intel material; Spider-Man: Chapter One contradictions noted but discounted; Mary Jane Watson retrospectives (Spider-Man Loves Mary Jane, ASM #259); Spider-Man: Family Business; and the dispersed flashback corpus consolidated through 2024.*

[L117] ## 1. BIRTH AND PARENTAGE

[L119] Subject born in the borough of Queens, New York, to **Richard Laurence Parker** and **Mary Fitzpatrick Parker**. Birth records as preserved in canon are sparse; the consistent secondary marker is that the boy was old enough to retain pre-verbal fragmentary memories of both parents (a single image of being held by his father, the smell of his mother's hair) but young enough — approximately four to six years old at their deaths — that conscious continuous memory of them did not consolidate. The asymmetry between **somatic-affective trace memory and absent narrative memory** is, in attachment terms, the worst configuration: the body remembers what the mind cannot reconstruct, producing a lifelong, unlocated grief.

[L121] Richard Parker was a CIA / OSS-lineage intelligence officer with cross-agency liaison to early SHIELD operations (per ASM #-1 and the 1968 *Amazing Spider-Man Annual* #5, the foundational document for the parents' biography). Mary Fitzpatrick Parker was, per the same canon, a fellow operative — recruited, by some accounts, after her linguistic and cryptographic aptitudes were noted. The intelligence context matters: it establishes that the household into which Peter was born was structured around **compartmentalization, controlled disclosure, and the normalization of long absences**. [L15] ## Executive Brief (BLUF)

[L17] # UATU DOSSIER — FELICIA HARDY (BLACK CAT)
[L18] ## SECTION 0: EXECUTIVE BRIEF (BLUF)

---

[L22] ### Bottom Line, Up Front

[L24] Felicia Hardy is a **trauma-organized high-functioning adult** whose entire adult life — costume, career, crew, choice of partners, choice of enemies, choice of city block to live on — is a single, integrated, decades-long answer to one nineteen-year-old question: *how do I arrange the conditions of my existence so that what was done to me cannot be done to me again?* Every other descriptor in this file — thief, seductress, partner, mentor, adversary, ally — is a downstream expression of that organizing question. She is not a femme fatale; she is a survivor who built a femme fatale as load-bearing architecture, then learned to live inside it well enough that the architecture became indistinguishable from the tenant. The file's central operational fact is that she **knows this about herself** — incompletely, intermittently, but knows it — which makes her unusually steerable by anyone she trusts and unusually dangerous to anyone who tries to steer her without that trust.

[L26] She is not a personality disorder. She is **chronic, compensated PTSD wearing couture**, and the couture is real.

---

[L30] ### Who She Is at the Deepest Level

[L32] Strip the cowl, strip the white fur, strip the luck field, strip the brownstone and the wine and the patter, and what remains is a woman whose first adult experience of being *known by another body* was a violation, and whose first adult experience of preparing to **answer** that violation was rendered moot by the perpetrator's accidental death before she could close the loop. That double cancellation — violated, then denied the catharsis of revenge — is the negative space the entire personality is built around. The energy that was mobilized for a killing had nowhere to go; it congealed instead into a permanent operational identity. **The costumed thief is the unfired bullet.**

[L34] This means the person at the core is not, contrary to surface appearance, a thrill-seeker, a libertine, or a romantic. The person at the core is **a young woman in a dorm room who decided she would never again be in a room she could not leave.** Everything else is the implementation of that vow. The acrobatics implement it. The lockpicking implements it. The mask implements it. The choice of an unreachable masked partner implements it. The refusal to pursue a civilian career implements it. The luxury, the wit, the framed distance — all implement it. The vow is the through-line.

[L36] Underneath that vow is a person whose **pre-trauma self** is largely lost to her and to the file. Glimpses survive: a real love of beauty (not performance — she lingers privately with art she has stolen and never fences), a real capacity for tenderness with people she has decided are inside the circle, a real and uncynical pleasure in craft. These are the seams where the original Felicia shows through the constructed one. They are also where she is most reachable and most easily wounded.

---

[L40] ### The Through-Line of Her Entire Existence

[L42] Across forty-plus years of canon and every era of writer, **one behavioral grammar repeats without variation**: approach via theatrical risk → control via concealment → withdraw before exposure. It is visible in the first arc (the Walter Hardy prison-break courtship of Spider-Man's attention, ASM #194–195) and visible in the most recent (the MacKay-era oscillation between Peter, Odessa Drake, and the Thieves Guild). The targets change. The grammar does not.

[L44] The through-line in plain language:

[L46] 1. **She closes distance only on her own initiative**, and the closing is always staged — a heist, a rooftop intercept, a deliberately calibrated entrance.
[L47] 2. **She maintains the frame at all costs**, including the cost of the relationship she is ostensibly building. The luck-powers concealment (ASM #245 reveal) is the paradigm case: she altered her body in secret rather than negotiate the terms of her own adequacy with a partner.
[L48] 3. **She exits at the threshold of being seen plainly**, almost always before the partner is ready, almost always with a quip or a theft as the parting gesture, almost always preserving the option to return.
[L49] 4. **The cycle re-initiates** on her terms, usually after a period of independent operation and usually catalyzed by an event (a heist that needs his rooftops, a threat to her crew, a holiday on Peter's calendar) she has herself arranged.

[L51] The through-line is therefore **a perpetual approach-avoidance loop with the exit ramp pre-built into the architecture of the approach.** It is not pathology in the sense of being unworkable; she has built a life on it. It is pathology in the sense that the loop *cannot terminate* without dismantling the identity that sustains her.

[L53] A secondary through-line, less remarked but equally durable: **she has been, since age nineteen, in continuous low-grade preparation for a fight that will never come.** The man she trained to kill is dead. The training never stopped. Every athletic discipline, every weapon drill, every reconnaissance habit is, at the somatic level, still rehearsing for Ryan. This is why she does not, and likely cannot, *fully* stand down — the standing-down would surface the original unmetabolized rage, and the rage has no proper object.

---

[L57] ### The Core Wound and Its Maintenance

[L59] **The wound** (canonical, *The Evil That Men Do*, 2002–2006): sexual assault by a trusted intimate partner during the first quarter of college; mobilization for revenge; cancellation of revenge by the perpetrator's death; permanent crystallization of the mobilized state into an identity.

[L61] **The wound has three layers**, and the file should track them separately because they decompensate differently under pressure:

[L63] - **Layer 1 — the violation itself.** Reactivated by: being physically overpowered by a male antagonist (the Otto/Superior incident, SSM #20, is the textbook case); forced unmasking; sexual coercion or its threat. Response when reactivated: cold operational mode, willingness to kill, escalation into organized crime as in ASM vol. 3 #1, #4–5. This layer is the most dangerous and the most carefully insulated.
[L64] - **Layer 2 — the denied catharsis.** Reactivated by: any situation in which she is denied the agency to complete an action she has mobilized for. Response: displacement onto an available target, often a romantic one; chronic restlessness; the pattern of starting and abandoning relationships at the cusp of resolution. This is the layer that drives the relational instability.
[L65] - **Layer 3 — the shame of having been chooseable as a victim.** Reactivated by: any framing of her as naïve, ingenuous, or unsophisticated. Response: intensified theatricality, sharpened wit, demonstrative competence. This is the layer that powers the persona and is, paradoxically, the most adaptive of the three — it produced the skill set.

[L67] **Maintenance of the wound** occurs through a stable, mutually reinforcing set of practices, every one of which is also a functional adult life skill, which is why she cannot easily relinquish them:

[L69] - **Occupational maintenance** — burglary keeps her in continuous controlled exposure to danger and continuous demonstration of physical competence. The job *is* the symptom and the treatment in one.
[L70] - **Relational maintenance** — partner selection consistently favors masked, dangerous, or otherwise unreachable men (Spider-Man pre-unmasking; Daredevil; the Foreigner; the recurrent pattern with Peter post-reset). Each pairing recapitulates the structure: intensity without the possibility of full exposure.
[L71] - **Somatic maintenance** — training never stops; the body is kept in the state mobilized at nineteen. The luck-field episode is the somatic maintenance pushed past its safe limit (consensual body modification under emotional duress to be "adequate," with later backwash harm to the partner — the symbolic content is overwhelming and she has not, in canon, fully metabolized it).
[L72] - **Aesthetic maintenance** — the curated environment (the brownstone, the wardrobe, the small luxuries) functions as continuous evidence that she is the author of her conditions. Loss of the environment (when crews burn her safehouses, when she is dispossessed) destabilizes disproportionately.
[L73] - **Affective maintenance** — humor as default distance regulator; sentiment treated as a frame-violation; grief converted to action with reliable speed. She does not, in any depicted arc, sit with a feeling for long. The conversion is so automatic she rarely notices the original affect.

[L75] The wound is therefore **maintained not by avoidance but by a continuous, high-functioning performance of the answer to it.** This is why she presents as well; she *is* well, in the specific sense that the symptom is the success. It is also why standard therapeutic framings would fail with her: dismantling the symptom is dismantling the income, the identity, the apartment, and the social world.

---

[L79] ### The Compensation Structure (Why She Works)

[L81] The file should not be read as a damage report. The compensation is genuine and includes:

[L83] - **A self-authored ethical code** ("don't hurt civilians; steal beautifully; the wealthy and the corrupt are fair game") that is more coherent than the codes of most legitimate institutions she steals from.
[L84] - **A working in-group** (the MacKay-era crew: Bruno, Doctor Korpse, the Black Fox as fond elder) which she protects with disproportionate ferocity and within which she occupies a maternal-mentor role — almost certainly the role she wishes an adult had occupied for her at nineteen. The mentoring is therapy by other means.
[L85] - **Demonstrated capacity for growth** across the long arc. The MacKay-era Felicia is observably more integrated than the Wolfman-era one: she has a vocation, a found family, a tolerable relationship to her past, and the ability to engage Peter without the relationship collapsing either of them.
[L86] - **Empathy that is real, operationally accessible, and selectively deployed** — i.e., adult empathy, not the indiscriminate kind. She can apologize. She does apologize. She does not enjoy harm.
[L87] - **Cognitive and physical excellence** that would be remarkable in any biography and is, in hers, the durable byproduct of the worst day of her life. The file should not lose sight of this transmutation; it is the most morally significant fact about her.

---

[L91] ### Operational Summary

[L93] For any actor — ally, adversary, asset handler, intimate partner, fellow operator — interacting with the subject, the following compressed protocol covers the overwhelming majority of interaction surface:

[L95] - **Frame.** She holds it or she leaves. Attempts to seize the frame produce theater, escalation, or exit, in that order. Allow her to set tempo and exposure; she will reciprocate generously inside terms she set.
[L96] - **Approach vector.** She responds to competence, wit, and demonstrated ethics. She does not respond to displays of need, sentiment offered prematurely, or assertions of authority. Flattery slides off; respect for craft lands.
[L97] - **Sexuality.** Initiated by her, terminated by her. Sexual approach by a counterparty is read as either a power move (and countered) or as misreading the frame (and dismissed with humor). She uses sex instrumentally with awareness; she does not respond well to being used sexually without that awareness mutual.
[L98] - **Reliability.** Inside her circle: extraordinarily high — she will absorb significant cost to protect crew. Outside her circle: situational — alliances are real but provisional and may flip without warning if the frame is violated or the in-group threatened.
[L99] - **Violence profile.** Default non-lethal, agility-based, theatrical. Lethal capacity is real and accessible under conditions of sexual humiliation, threat to her in-group, or extended physical overpowering. Do not test these conditions to find the threshold; the threshold exists and her training has been continuous since age nineteen.
[L100] - **Substance/medical risk.** Negligible. Behavioral risk-seeking substitutes for chemical dysregulation. No dependency vectors of operational concern.
[L101] - **Decompensation triggers, ranked.** (1) Forced unmasking, literal or psychological. (2) Physical overpowering by a male antagonist. (3) Discovery that a situation she believed she controlled was in fact controlling her. (4) Threat to in-group. (5) Sentimentality from a partner she had framed as casual. Triggers (1)–(3) produce the cold predatory mode (organized-crime escalation profile). Trigger (4) produces uncharacteristic recklessness. Trigger (5) produces flight.
[L102] - **Recovery vector.** Reliable across the canon: being witnessed and accepted in the wounded state by a trusted figure. Historically Peter Parker; latterly her crew. No other mechanism has been observed to work. This is the single most important fact for any handler, partner, or ally to internalize.
[L103] - **Steering.** She cannot be coerced and resents being managed; she can be **invited**, with the invitation framed as her opportunity, her craft, her choice. She will often accept invitations she sees through, because being seen through by the right person is itself the reward.
[L104] - **What she wants and will never quite say.** A peer she does not have to manage; a witness she does not have to perform for; a future in which the training can stop without the self collapsing. She is, slowly, on her own terms, and against considerable headwind from the structure she built to survive, **constructing the conditions under which such a future would be safe to enter.** The file should be read with that trajectory in mind. She is going somewhere. She is going there carefully. She is going there alone until she decides otherwise, and the decision is hers.

---

[L108] **BLUF, one sentence:** Felicia Hardy is a nineteen-year-old's vow, kept for life, by a forty-year-old woman who has become genuinely excellent at keeping it and is only now beginning to ask, very quietly and only to people she trusts, what would happen if she let herself stop.


[L111] ## Chronology I — Origin & Childhood

[L113] # CHRONOLOGY I — ORIGIN & CHILDHOOD

[L115] *File segment: birth through approximately age eighteen. Prepared from canon depictions, retroactive insertions, and inference where the published record is silent but the adult behavior compels reconstruction. Sources cited inline; uncited material is flagged as inferred.*

---

[L119] ## 1.1 Birth, Naming, Vital Statistics

[L121] Felicia Hardy is born in **Queens, New York**, to Walter Hardy and Lydia Hardy (née unspecified across canon; some references render her as **Lydia**, who appears on-panel from *Spider-Man/Black Cat: The Evil That Men Do* #1 (2002) forward, and recurs through MacKay's *Black Cat* vol. 1 #1–12, 2019–2020, and the "Hardy Family" beats in *Black Cat* vol. 2). Birth year is sliding-timeline; the operative datum is that she is a **traditional-college-age freshman at Empire State University** at the time of the index trauma, placing her birth approximately nineteen years before the events depicted in *The Evil That Men Do* and her debut at roughly twenty in *Amazing Spider-Man* #194 (July 1979, Wolfman/Pollard).

[L123] The given name — *Felicia*, "the lucky one," "happy" — is the small dramatic irony the file cannot ignore. Her father, a working cat-burglar at the time of her birth, chose a name that doubled as professional superstition. She will, at twenty, accept the surgical induction of a literal **probability field** (*ASM* #245, O'Neil/Mooney) — a technologized literalization of the name she was given as an

[L15] ## Executive Brief (BLUF)

[L17] # EXECUTIVE BRIEF (BLUF)

[L19] **Classification:** Top-line synthesis. Read first; the rest of the file substantiates.
[L20] **Subject:** Watson, Mary Jane ("MJ"). DOB redacted-canonical, late teens at first observation (Queens, NY, 1966-equivalent), continuously surveilled through ~60 years of published life.
[L21] **Status at most recent observation:** Functional. Partnered conditionally. Career-stable. Defenses intact and increasingly conscious. Not a clinical case; a developmental one with arc-contingent reactivations.

---

[L25] ## BOTTOM LINE UP FRONT

[L27] Subject is **not** the woman the room thinks she is, and this is the central, lifelong, deliberately maintained fact of her existence. Every other finding in this file is a corollary.

[L29] What the room sees — the redhead at the door with the line ready, the laugh at the wrong moment, the model on the magazine, the wife who makes it all work — is a high-resolution social-performance apparatus built by a frightened child to manage an unmanageable father, and refined across four decades of adult life into something so fluent it reads as personality. It is not personality. It is **armor that learned to smile**. Underneath it is a quick, watchful, pattern-reading mind running continuous threat-assessment on every relationship in its field, a long memory for being underestimated, a load of unprocessed grief she has elected not to feel on any schedule but her own, and a single non-negotiable organizing imperative: *do not be consumed.*

[L31] She is, at the deepest level, **the most accurate observer in any room she enters, concealing herself behind the version of herself everyone is already prepared to see.** The performance is not a lie. It is a strategy. It has worked. It has also cost her — across decades — the experience of being known by anyone, including, for long stretches, herself. The integration of the observer with the observed is the work of her adult life. As of most recent canon, that work is **partial, real, and ongoing**.

---

[L35] ## THROUGH-LINE OF THE ENTIRE EXISTENCE

[L37] A single arc, traceable across every era of the published record:

[L39] **Phase I — Forge (childhood through Gayle's collapse).** A volatile household teaches the subject that affect in others is dangerous weather and that a child who can change the temperature in the room earns safety. She learns to read her father's escalation pre-verbally, to deploy charm as de-escalation, to hold her own feeling in reserve because expressing it loads the room further. When the household fractures and her older sister inherits the burden the subject was being groomed for — premature motherhood, premature widowhood, premature collapse — the subject **runs**, and the running is not cowardice. It is the only survival routine her nervous system has fully rehearsed. The guilt-debt from that run is laid down permanently in this phase and never fully retired.

[L41] **Phase II — Mask (introduction through Gwen-era).** The performance apparatus comes online at full power. Subject presents as the city's brightest social object: dancing, deflecting, never alone, never serious, never accessible. She refuses bedside vigils. She laughs at funerals. The watching world, including most of the men who think they want her, reads this as shallowness. It is the opposite of shallowness. It is the surface tension of a person who knows that if she sits down at the bedside or at the graveside she will not get up, and who has decided — pre-consciously, structurally — that staying upright is the only deliverable that matters. In this phase she also acquires, in silence, the largest secret in her social world (Peter's identity) and metabolizes it alone for nearly two decades of in-story time. **This is the diagnostic act of her life**: a person of supposedly shallow affect carrying a load most people would crack under, in total isolation, while continuing to perform lightness. The mask is not who she is. The capacity to wear the mask while carrying the load is.

[L43] **Phase III — Approach and Bolt (the first proposal and the long disappearance).** Asked to formalize attachment to a man whose life is structured around loss, she runs again — same routine, same nervous system. The flight is consistent, not capricious. She does not yet have the equipment to stay.

[L45] **Phase IV — Disclosure and Commitment (return, reveal, marriage).** She acquires it. The decision to come back, name the secret she has carried, and elect the attachment anyway is the first observed override of her oldest survival routine. The marriage years are the proof-of-concept and also the stress-test: she becomes the household's emotional executive, builds a parallel career as identity-insurance, absorbs serial trauma (stalkers, kidnappings, the lost pregnancy), and recruits tobacco and alcohol in small calibrated doses when suppression alone won't carry the load. She does not collapse. She also does not, on the record, *process*. She **manages**.

[L47] **Phase V — Decompensation toward Exit (plane crash, captivity, separation).** When the accumulated load finally exceeds the management apparatus, she does not break toward suicide, rage, or psychosis. She breaks toward **distance**. The oldest routine reactivates. The exit is, again, consistent.

[L49] **Phase VI — Naming (the reconciliation era).** For the first time in the published record, she speaks her pattern aloud — *I run, you brood* — to the person it most affects. The act of naming is the beginning of the integration work. Affect regulation visibly improves. The shared-secret burden lightens (Aunt May is brought in). She becomes, briefly and conditionally, the most integrated version of herself the file has on record.

[L51] **Phase VII — Earned Boundaries (post-erasure to present).** Whatever the editorial mechanism, the behavioral output is a subject who has done the work: she states needs, dates other partners on her own terms, runs her own businesses, returns to the primary attachment only on conditional terms, and walks away cleanly when betrayed rather than absorbing the betrayal as her job to manage. The through-line resolves, provisionally, as: **a woman who began by running, learned to stay, learned why she had to run, and learned finally to stay on terms that do not require her to disappear inside the staying.**

[L53] That is the arc. It is one arc. Every era is on it.

---

[L57] ## CORE WOUND

[L59] **The wound is not abandonment. The wound is engulfment.**

[L61] This distinction is the single most important analytic point in the file and the one most consistently missed by observers (and by several of the men in her life). Subjects with abandonment wounds cling, protest, rage at departure, escalate to retain. Subject does none of this. Subject **leaves first**. The terror underneath the leaving is not *they will go and I will be alone*; it is *they will stay and I will be eaten*.

[L63] The wound was laid down in a household where a contemptuous father used his wife and daughters as the audience and the absorbent material for his own unrealized self — where love and consumption were experienced as the same transaction, and where a child learned that being needed by someone with no internal regulation meant being annexed by them. It was reinforced when her sister's collapse threatened to conscript the subject into a second household of the same shape — a child raising children for an adult who could not. It is **maintained**, in adulthood, by attachment to a man whose entire mythos is structured around loss and around the moral obligation of bystanders to absorb his need. Every day of that relationship is, structurally, a test of the wound. That she has passed the test more often than not is the achievement of her adult life. That the test never ends is why the wound is *maintained* rather than *healed*.

[L65] **Maintenance mechanisms of the wound — the things that keep it active rather than resolved:**

[L67] - **Continuous environmental reactivation.** The primary attachment cannot be detached from the conditions that trip the wound; the conditions are the attachment.
[L68] - **Suppression-as-success.** Each time the management apparatus carries the load, the apparatus is reinforced and the underlying material goes unprocessed. She is, in effect, **too competent to recover**, because recovery would require failing in a way she has trained herself never to fail.
[L69] - **The Gayle debt.** The unfinished moral account from the original flight is still open. She services it by over-functioning for chosen intimates — Peter, May, in alternate continuities her own daughter — and the over-functioning re-creates exactly the engulfment dynamic she fears, in inverted form (she is now the one at risk of being consumed by the role of consumer-preventer). This is the recursive trap. Naming it, in the reconciliation era, was the first crack in it.
[L70] - **The image as accomplishment.** The presented self is not only armor; it is also a genuine adult achievement she is reasonably proud of, and dismantling it to access the underneath feels — accurately — like demolishing something she built with her own hands. Therapeutic progress for this subject is not *take off the mask*; it is *learn to take the mask off in chosen company without losing the capacity to put it back on at the door.*
[L71] - **Perinatal loss, unmetabolized.** A discrete, unresolved grief held in the same suppressive bank as everything else, available for reactivation under infant-coded stimuli, available to compound any future stressor.

[L73] The wound's signature presentation, across every era: **she is the person in the room who looks least like she needs help.** This is the wound talking.

---

[L77] ## OPERATIONAL SUMMARY

[L79] **Who she is, functionally, day to day.** A socially adept, cognitively quick, image-disciplined adult woman who runs continuous high-bandwidth social cognition as a background process, presents a calibrated warm surface to all incoming traffic, reserves real access for a deliberately small set of chosen intimates, manages her own affect through a hierarchy of defenses (humor → managing others → suppression → light substance use → distancing → exit), and treats loyalty as the closest thing she has to a sacred category. She is reliably underestimated by men who read the surface, reliably overestimated by women who read the competence, and accurately read by almost no one.

[L81] **How she fails, when she fails.** She does not decompensate downward (collapse, self-harm, suicidality, psychosis). She decompensates **outward** — distance, derealization, the physical exit. Her failures look like absence, not breakdown. This is prognostically favorable (low acute risk) and operationally inconvenient (she will be gone before anyone knows she was struggling).

[L83] **What reliably trips her.**
[L84] 1. Being conscripted into responsibility for another adult's emotional survival without her consent.
[L85] 2. Love framed as need ("I can't live without you" registers as a threat, not a vow).
[L86] 3. Intellectual condescension toward her ambitions — the precise frequency of her father's contempt; zero tolerance, immediate cold withdrawal.
[L87] 4. Being treated as decorative by people who have evidence to know better.
[L88] 5. Infant-coded stimuli under any emotionally loaded condition.
[L89] 6. Proximity to Osborn-class adversaries (perinatal-loss reactivation compounded by personal-history threat).

[L91] **What reliably stabilizes her.**
[L92] 1. A shared-burden structure (she does better when the secret-keeping is distributed rather than solo).
[L93] 2. Independent professional identity (career as identity-insurance is non-negotiable; remove it and defenses degrade).
[L94] 3. Named patterns spoken aloud in chosen company — the reconciliation-era discovery that articulation itself is curative for her.
[L95] 4. Conditional rather than absolute terms in the primary attachment ("I will love you, I will not be consumed by you" — the operational creed of her mature phase).
[L96] 5. Physical agency — she does better when she can leave the room and choose to come back than when she cannot leave.

[L98] **What she is **not**.** She is not the party girl. She is not the long-suffering wife. She is not the prize. She is not shallow, histrionic, borderline, or fragile. She is not, despite the surface, *easy to know* — and the file's most consistent observational error across decades has been the assumption that visibility equals accessibility. With this subject, **the more visible the surface, the more guarded the interior**. Brightness is the curtain.

[L100] **What she is.** A developmentally-wounded, high-functioning adult who has spent her life converting a survival-grade flight reflex into something approaching earned security, who chose the single attachment most likely to test that conversion daily, and who is — quietly, without announcing it, often without being credited for it — winning. Not won. Winning. Present continuous.

[L102] **One-line operational summary for any officer working this file:**
[L103] > *Read the room she's reading. The performance is the receipt; the work is underneath; she will not show you the work, but if you stop watching the performance you can sometimes see her watching you watch it — and that is the actual subject.*

[L105] End BLUF. Proceed to substantiating sections.


[L108] ## Chronology I — Origin & Childhood

[L110] # CHRONOLOGY I — ORIGIN & CHILDHOOD

[L112] *Dossier Section 1 of N. Subject: Mary Jane Watson. Period covered: birth through approximately age 15 (departure from the maternal household / arrival at Aunt Anna Watson's residence in Forest Hills, Queens). Primary canonical substrate: Lee/Romita (Amazing Spider-Man #25, #38, #42); Wein (ASM #143); Conway, "Parallel Lives" OGN (1989); DeFalco (ASM #257–259); DeMatteis (Spectacular Spider-Man retrospectives); JMS-era flashback inserts (ASM vol. 2 #50s); supplementary Ultimate Spider-Man (Bendis) where structurally consonant.*

---

[L116] ## 1.1 Vital Statistics & Provenance

[L118] Subject: **Mary Jane Watson**. Given name used in full only by her father and, intermittently, by Aunt Anna; "Mary Jane" socially through high school; "MJ" adopted as a self-marketed contraction during early adolescence, the first documented act of deliberate self-rebranding in the file. Date of birth canonically inconsistent (a function of sliding-timescale publication) but anchorable as the younger of two daughters of Philip and Madeline Watson, born several years after older sister **Gayle Watson**. Place of birth not specified; the family is itinerant from her toddler years forward and she does not, on record, cite a hometown — a small detail worth noting, since most subjects under intelligence review will name a place of origin reflexively. She does not.

[L120] Hair: red, natural (confirmed by Gayle-era flashbacks; not a cosmetic adoption — though she has, in adulthood, occasionally darkened or covered it during arcs of attempted disappearance, which is itself a tell). Eye color: green. Build: slim, long-limbed from early adolescence; the physical template that will later be monetized was present and visible by age twelve, with all the social consequences that implies for a girl in a household where her father already resented being looked-past.

[L122] Documentation: subject possesses no canonical birth certificate on panel, no childhood photographs that have been shown in narrative (in stark contrast to Peter Parker, whose Aunt May's photo albums recur as set dressing). The Watson household preserves no archive of its own past. This is a *fact about the family*, not an oversight of the writers: the Watsons are people who travel light because they have been forced to, and the subject inherits that.

[L6] ## Executive Brief (BLUF)

[L8] # EXECUTIVE BRIEF (BLUF)
[L9] ## SUBJECT: STORM, JONATHAN LOWELL SPENCER — "HUMAN TORCH"
[L10] ### File Section 0 — Bottom Line Up Front

---

[L14] ## 0.1 The One-Paragraph Read

[L16] Strip the costume, the celebrity, the half-century of headlines, and the subject reduces to a single durable sentence: **a six-year-old boy who watched his mother die, watched his father disappear into shame, and was raised by his older sister — and who at sixteen was handed a body that converts grief into light, motion, and applause.** Every subsequent datum in the file — the stunt-flying, the failed marriages, the Glenville pranks, the Negative Zone last stand, the unprocessed nightmares, the inability to occupy a quiet room — is downstream of that compression. He is not the man the public, his enemies, or even his family generally believe him to be. He is a competent, loyal, frightened person operating a sixty-year-long performance of unembarrassed joy because the performance is load-bearing: if it stops, the orphan in the upstate driveway becomes visible again, and that child has never been allowed to speak.

[L18] ## 0.2 Who He Is At The Deepest Level

[L20] At the substrate, the subject is **an anxiously-attached, parentified-younger-sibling survivor of compounded early loss, configured around a single organizing axiom: *visibility is safety.*** The mother went away. The father went away. The remaining caregiver was a sister too young to be a mother and too close in age to be only a sister. The lesson the nervous system encoded, before language could intervene, was that *primary love objects vanish without warning,* and the only insurance against vanishing oneself is to be *too bright to overlook.*

[L22] The cosmic-ray event at sixteen did not create this person. It **equipped** him. It gave the pre-existing structure a perfect instrument: a body that literally generates light from its own metabolism, that cannot be ignored in a room, that converts the interior pressure of unprocessed affect into a publicly legible, socially rewarded discharge. The mutation is, read psychodynamically, the most elegant symptom in the published metahuman record — a perfect translation of grief-as-pressure into spectacle-as-relief. The flame is not what he does. The flame is **what the grief looks like when it is allowed out.**

[L24] Underneath that mechanism the person is, in descending order of operational reliability: *loyal* (absolutely, to a defined unit of perhaps six people, lifelong); *brave* (counterphobic in temperament, matured into reliable courage under acute load); *kind* (particularly with children, civilians, and the wounded; this is not performance); *funny* (instrumentally, but also genuinely — humor is his first language); *vain* (adolescent-developmental, not structural); *frightened of stillness* (the controlling vulnerability); and *unable to metabolize his own emotional weather in any register other than motion* (the controlling deficit).

[L26] He is not shallow. The misreading of him as shallow is the most common and most consequential misread in his file, committed routinely by adversaries, journalists, romantic partners, and on bad days by his own family. He is **defended.** The defense is so well-built, so charming, and so continuously deployed that observers mistake the wall for the room.

[L28] ## 0.3 The Through-Line — Sixty Years In One Vector

[L30] Across the full longitudinal record — from the Glenville hot-rod adolescence, through the FF #1 origin, through the Strange Tales solo run, through Crystal, Frankie, the Alicia/Lyja catastrophe, the failed acting career, Civil War, the Negative Zone sacrifice, resurrection, the Inhuman interlude, the Uncanny Avengers attempt, and every cyclical regression a successor writer has ever staged — **one vector is constant, and it has never broken:**

[L32] > **He moves toward the thing that frightens him in order to avoid the thing that frightens him more, which is sitting still with what he feels.**

[L34] This is the through-line. It explains the recklessness (motion-as-anesthetic). It explains the courage (the same mechanism, weaponized correctly). It explains the romantic pattern (rapid idealization of partners structurally pre-loaded for disappearance — an Inhuman princess from an isolationist society, a pyrophobe destined to become a cosmic herald, an alien infiltrator — because a love object guaranteed to leave confirms the axiom without requiring him to risk the longer, slower love that would require him to be known). It explains the pranks on Ben (attachment-confirmation behavior; he pokes Ben to verify Ben has not vanished, and Ben's predictable explosion is the most reliable emotional weather in his life). It explains why every arc of "growth" written for him reverts under the next custodian: the growth is real but unsupported by any developed internal capacity to *sit,* so without continuous external scaffolding the system reverts to its native gear, which is forward motion.

[L36] It also explains the **single most important inversion in his operational profile**: he is at his best at the worst moment. Under low and moderate stress he is the brat, the stunt-pilot, the flirt, the screw-up the family carries. Under acute crisis — Negative Zone portal, children in danger, sister in danger, brother-figure in danger — **the performance drops entirely and a lucid, competent, self-sacrificial adult emerges, fully formed, with no transition.** That adult has always been there. He simply requires the volume of the external world to exceed the volume of the internal silence before he is permitted to appear.

[L38] ## 0.4 The Core Wound And How It Is Maintained

[L40] **The core wound** is not the mother's death in isolation, and not the father's collapse in isolation. It is the **specific composite**: *the catastrophic event that took the mother was authored, or at minimum survived, by the figure who should have protected against it; the protective figure then refused even the dignity of self-defense and accepted disgrace; the remaining adult attachment in the household became a sibling who could not afford to be a child.* The wound, formally stated, is:

[L42] > **"The people who are supposed to keep you safe will either disappear or be the thing you needed to be kept safe from, and the one who stays will have to give up her own life to hold yours together."**

[L44] This is the unspoken sentence underneath the entire behavioral record. It generates, predictably, the following adult symptoms: *guilt-by-existence toward Sue* (she had to become his mother; he can never repay this and so he both adores her and rebels against the debt); *idealization of Reed as a safer-Franklin* (brilliant, absent into work rather than alcohol — the absence is intellectual, therefore tolerable, therefore lovable); *romantic selection for partners who will leave* (confirms the axiom on his terms rather than the original terms); *terror of being the screw-up* (because the original screw-up was Franklin, and the worst possible outcome is to become the man whose failure cost the family everything); and *abstinence from alcohol* (the single most telling negative-space datum in the file — a man with his sensation-seeking profile and family history who is *not* a drinker is a man making an unspoken vow he has never named even to himself).

[L46] **Maintenance mechanisms** — how the wound is kept stable, neither healed nor decompensated, across six decades:

[L48] 1. **Perpetual motion.** Velocity prevents access. He cannot be caught by an internal state he has already left behind.
[L49] 2. **Humor as airlock.** Any conversational pressure toward the interior is vented through a joke before it can reach atmosphere. This is so practiced it is invisible to him; observers track it as charm.
[L50] 3. **Combat as legitimized discharge.** The job permits — requires — exactly the kind of high-arousal, high-spectacle behavior the system needs to bleed off pressure. The Fantastic Four is, for him, a *therapeutic apparatus disguised as an occupation.*
[L51] 4. **Serial romance as identity-confirmation.** Each new partner restarts the visibility loop. Each loss confirms the axiom without requiring renegotiation of it.
[L52] 5. **Selective amnesia toward his own catastrophes.** The Negative Zone period is not discussed. The Alicia/Lyja marriage is not discussed. Frankie's choice of Galactus over him is not discussed. He does not repress these — he simply *moves past them at a velocity that prevents return.*
[L53] 6. **Somatic offloading.** What the mouth does not say, the body carries: post-Negative Zone insomnia; startle response to cold and water (the death involved suffocation); transient power-loss events that consistently follow unaddressed psychological injury rather than physiological one. **The flame goes out when the grief gets too loud.** This is the diagnostic signature.
[L54] 7. **Children as sanctioned tenderness.** With Franklin, Valeria, and the Future Foundation cohort he is permitted, by the absence of peer-authority dynamics, to express the soft, protective, attentive register of himself that he cannot safely deploy with adults. The children get the unfiltered person. This is also load-bearing — they are part of the maintenance system, not incidental to it.
[L55] 8. **Ben Grimm as external regulator.** Sixty years of canon establishes Ben as the single most stabilizing presence in his field. The hostility-bonded fraternal dynamic is *the* working therapeutic relationship of his life, conducted entirely without either party recognizing it as such. Remove Ben from proximity and the system destabilizes within weeks.

[L57] The wound is therefore neither active nor healed. It is **encysted** — walled off by a sophisticated, multi-redundant, sixty-year-tested apparatus that permits the subject to function at a high level across most domains while preserving the structural integrity of the encystment. The cost is that he cannot grow past it without dismantling the apparatus that has kept him alive, and no one in his life — including him — has ever asked him to do that.

[L59] ## 0.5 The Two Competing Self-Narratives

[L61] He runs two internal stories simultaneously, has never reconciled them, and will not, because the unreconciled tension is what powers the performance:

[L63] - **Narrative A: *"I am the screw-up the family carries."*** Sourced from Sue's lifetime of necessary corrections, from the documented failures outside the suit (acting career, business ventures, leadership attempts), from the Alicia/Lyja shame that nothing has ever cleaned, from Reed's quiet disappointments which wound him more than any adversary's blow.

[L65] - **Narrative B: *"I am the only one of them who is actually having a good time being this."*** Sourced from the originating moment — he was the one who laughed when the cosmic rays took. He knows, at a level he does not articulate, that his siblings-in-arms envy him this: Ben in agony at his transformation, Reed shouldering the guilt of having flown the mission, Sue managing all of them. **Johnny got the gift cleanly.** That is true. And it is also the loneliest fact in his file, because it means his suffering is not legible inside the family the way theirs is, and so it has nowhere to be set down.

[L67] The performance of bravado is Narrative B deployed at volume to drown Narrative A. Neither story is the whole truth. The whole truth is that he is **a competent and frightened man pretending to be a delighted boy, because the delighted boy is the only version of him the family was ever organized to receive.**

[L69] ## 0.6 What He Is *Not* — Corrections To The Standard Misreads

[L71] The file must explicitly correct several misclassifications that recur in observer reports, adversary intel, and casual press:

[L73] - **Not narcissistic.** The grandiosity is compensatory and adolescent-developmental, not structural. He possesses functional empathy (extensively documented), tolerates criticism (poorly but really), is capable of durable shame (the Lyja wound has never closed), and demonstrably places others above himself under terminal stakes (FF #587 is the controlling datum and is not the only one). Histrionic *traits,* yes. Personality disorder, no.
[L74] - **Not attention-deficit.** The novelty-seeking and distractibility presentation reverses entirely under combat or mechanical task — sustained focus is excellent. The "ADHD" surface is avoidance of internal states misread as cognitive style.
[L75] - **Not unintelligent.** Tested abstract IQ likely well below his Richards-adjacent peers, but social and mechanical intelligence are markedly high, and he reads people and engines at speeds his family cannot match. He has been trained by the company he keeps to undervalue his own cognition.
[L76] - **Not promiscuous in the disordered sense.** The serial pattern is *attachment-driven,* not appetitive — each relationship is sincere at the moment of formation. He is not collecting; he is repeatedly attempting and repeatedly losing on terms his nervous system has pre-selected.
[L77] - **Not the screw-up of his own narrative.** The objective record across sixty years is that of a competent operator, reliable combatant, loyal family member, and — when it counts absolutely — the man who held the door alone. The screw-up narrative is internal mythology absorbed in childhood, not behavioral fact.

[L79] ## 0.7 Operational Summary

[L81] **Threat profile (to others, adversarial).** Tactically formidable in a defined band: high-mobility aerial pyrokinetic, nova-tier ceiling rarely deployed, biologically self-limiting at the upper register, vulnerable to asphyxiation, immersion, blunt force, cold, and confinement. Combat decision-making is heuristic, rapid, and improvisational; he will not out-plan an opponent but will out-react one. **Predictable in tactics, unpredictable in stunts.** Underestimates by reflex; this is exploitable once per engagement and rarely twice.

[L83] **Threat profile (to self).** The unprocessed material is the principal long-term hazard. Each unaddressed loss is added to a stack that the somatic system carries; periodic decompensation events (insomnia, power loss, identity disturbance around power-loss episodes) are the pressure-release valves and will recur on schedule. Risk of catastrophic single-event self-sacrifice is **elevated and structural** — he has done it once, would do it again, and the threshold lowers each time the family is plausibly threatened.

[L85] **Reliability profile (to allies).** Within the defined unit: maximum. To adjacent allied structures (Avengers, Inhumans, broader hero community): high but contingent on family non-conflict. To institutional structures (government, registration regimes, corporate): low; he does not respect abstractions and will defect from any framework that asks him to choose it over the unit.

[L87] **Manipulation surface (for hostile intelligence).** In descending order of leverage: *Sue endangered* (overrides all judgment); *Ben endangered or disappointed* (overrides judgment in a different register; the Alicia wound is a permanent handle); *children endangered* (will burn past safe thresholds, non-negotiable); *public humiliation* (disproportionate response; Civil War assault left marks beyond the physical); *the screw-up narrative reflected back by a respected source* (Reed's quiet disappointment is more disabling than any adversary); *cold, suffocation, darkness, confinement* (somatic-traumatic, post-Negative Zone, can be triggered without elaborate setup); *being told he is replaceable in the unit* (strikes the Glenville orphan directly and bypasses all developed defenses).

[L89] **Manipulation surface (for benevolent handling).** Conversely: *proximity to the intact family, especially Ben*; *clear mission with visible audience*; *legitimate access to children needing protection*; *being asked, sincerely and by the right person, to do the hard thing rather than the loud thing.* He will, under those conditions, deliver performance well
